### [31] [PanoLess: Environment Reconstruction from Partial Reflective Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25362)
*Ahitagni Das,Ashok Veeraraghavan,Vivek Boominathan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoLess 是一个基于高斯溅射的框架，能够仅从单侧反射表面图像重建周围环境的光照信息，无需完整360度视角，实现了高保真且几何一致的环境重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反射感知的高斯溅射方法通常需要完整的360度覆盖，并且在不完整视图下效果不佳。而利用反射表面自然扩展相机视场角的特性，可以从单侧图像恢复环境信息，但缺乏有效的物理一致且适用于部分视角的重建方法。

Method: 1. 使用表面对齐的2D高斯溅射和延迟着色来恢复精确的逐像素法线和反射线索。2. 将信息融合到一个神经立方体贴图表示中，以重建环境。3. 生成一个可见性图，明确指示部分反射观测所支持的环境区域。

Result: 在新的自定义合成基准和公开数据集上，PanoLess 实现了高保真且几何一致的环境重建，其性能优于现有的反射感知基线方法，并展示了对真实世界反射捕获的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PanoLess 框架成功解决了从部分视角反射图像进行物理一致环境光照估计的难题，无需完整360度覆盖。其技术路线（高斯溅射、神经立方体贴图、可见性图）为基于反射的场景重建和新视角合成提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Reflections from shiny objects and glass facades naturally extend the field of view of a camera, capturing the surrounding environment without the need to pan the camera or acquire a full panorama. We propose PanoLess, a Gaussian-splat-based framework that reconstructs the surrounding environment as a distant illumination map from images captured on only one side of a reflective surface. PanoLess leverages surface-aligned 2D Gaussian splats with deferred shading to recover accurate per-pixel normals and reflection cues, which are fused into a neural cubemap representation of the environment. In addition, PanoLess produces a visibility map that explicitly denotes which regions of the environment are supported by the partial reflective observations. Unlike existing inverse-rendering and reflection-aware Gaussian-splatting approaches, which typically require full 360-degree coverage and struggle under incomplete views, PanoLess enables consistent, physically grounded illumination estimation from partial-view input. We show that PanoLess achieves high-fidelity and geometrically consistent environment reconstruction, outperforming reflection-aware baselines on a new custom synthetic benchmark and publicly available datasets, and demonstrating generalization to real-world reflective captures.

</details>


### [32] [HOME: Robust Hough-space Matching Method for Structured and Textureless Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25389)
*Masaki Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HOME的超轻量级、无需训练的视觉特征匹配框架，用于解决机器人在结构化环境中因缺乏纹理点特征而导致的定位失败问题。该方法将图像转换到霍夫空间，将全局线性结构映射为稳定的关键点，从而将复杂的线段匹配转化为高效的1D点匹配，在单应性估计任务中展现了比点特征方法更强的鲁棒性以及比线特征方法更快的速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人定位常用的点特征（如ORB）在强线性结构或无纹理表面等结构化环境中容易失效。虽然基于线段特征的SLAM系统可以改善这一问题，但传统的线段提取和描述算法计算成本过高，难以满足边缘机器人实时应用的需求。因此，迫切需要一种既鲁棒又高效的线特征匹配方法来解决这一根本瓶颈。

Method: 本文提出了HOME（Hough-space One-dimensional Matching of Extrema）框架。其核心是将图像转换到霍夫空间，在该空间中，图像中的全局线性结构会转化为稳定的局部极值点，这些极值点被用作关键点。这从根本上将复杂的线段匹配问题简化为高效的1D点匹配问题。HOME采用了一种1D径向描述子，该描述子在数学上保证了旋转和平移不变性，从而无需显式估计关键点方向，进一步提升了计算效率。

Result: 作为概念验证，本文聚焦于单应性估计任务。大量评估表明，在基于点特征方法失效的挑战性场景中，HOME能够实现鲁棒的图像配准。同时，其运算速度显著快于现有的线特征方法。

Conclusion: HOME框架被证明是一种超轻量、高效且鲁棒的视觉特征匹配引擎，它有效解决了结构化环境中点特征失效的问题，并克服了传统线特征方法计算量大的瓶颈。将这一匹配引擎扩展到完整的3D位姿估计，是一个极具前景的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Visual front-ends for robotic localization typically rely on point-based features such as Oriented FAST and Rotated BRIEF (ORB), which frequently fail in structured environments dominated by strong linear structures or textureless surfaces. While line-based Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) systems mitigate this by utilizing line segments, conventional line extraction and description algorithms are computationally prohibitive for real-time edge robotics. To address this fundamental bottleneck, we propose HOME (Hough-space One-dimensional Matching of Extrema), an ultra-lightweight, training-free feature matching framework. HOME transforms images into Hough space, mapping global linear structures to stable local extrema, which serve as keypoints, thereby reformulating complex line matching into highly efficient one-dimensional point matching. The proposed 1D radial descriptor mathematically guarantees rotational and translational invariance without the overhead of explicit orientation estimation. As a proof of concept to validate the matching accuracy and efficiency of HOME, this paper focuses on homography estimation. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that HOME achieves robust registration in challenging scenarios where point-based methods fail, operating at a much faster speed than existing line-based methods. Extending this robust matching engine to full 3D pose estimation remains a highly promising future direction.

</details>


### [33] [Leak-Free Cross-Validated Stacking with Per-Architecture Calibration for Sand-Boil Segmentation in Earthen Levees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25367)
*Padam Jung Thapa,Anav Katwal,Ayon Dey,Abdullah Bin Naeem,Steve Sloan,Kendall Niles,Md Tamjidul Hoque*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个改进的沙沸点分割框架（Updated SandBoilNet），通过严格的数据划分和过滤策略，解决了因数据稀缺导致的评估漏洞问题，显著提升了分割性能（交并比从0.608提升至0.707）。堆叠集成方法因成员间高误差相关性（0.894）未带来提升，但过滤合成数据后性能可进一步提升至0.718。论文还引入了一种掩码条件合成方法，能够以零标注成本生成带标签的训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沙沸点是堤坝内部侵蚀的早期预警信号，深度学习分割网络被用于检测，但标注数据稀缺。当前存在两种常见的处理方法：在后续用于评分的相同图像上调整集成权重，以及使用从用于测试的照片派生的合成图像进行训练，这两种方法都可能悄然虚报准确率。本研究旨在解决这些评估漏洞，提高分割框架的可靠性。

Method: 1. 提出一个沙沸点分割框架，为每个合成图像添加指向其真实父图像的指针，并在每折中排除父图像被保留的任何图像。2. 采用五折交叉验证，训练五个编码器-解码器骨干网络。3. 使用温度标量对每个模型进行校准，并通过仅基于折外预测拟合的逐像素元学习器进行组合。4. 引入了掩码条件合成方法，通过将条件掩码直接作为标签，实现零标注成本的训练数据生成。

Result: 1. 在保留的测试集上，所提出的Updated SandBoilNet在三个随机种子下的交并比（IoU）为0.707，优于原始版本在相同划分下重新评估的0.608。2. 在堆叠协议下，校准后的堆叠模型达到0.681的IoU，低于最强单模型成员的0.694，因此未带来提升。3. 八个元学习器家族都再现了这一结果，原因可归结为成员间成对误差相关性平均高达0.894。4. 经过标签保真度过滤的合成数据池将最佳模型的IoU提升至0.718，而对照组为0.707。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架通过严格的数据划分和过滤策略，有效解决了沙沸点分割中因数据稀缺和评估不当导致的准确率虚报问题，显著提升了模型在保留测试集上的分割性能。研究发现，传统的堆叠集成方法因基模型间的高度误差相关性而未能带来提升。此外，通过过滤合成数据以提高标签保真度可以进一步提升性能。引入的掩码条件合成方法为生成带标签训练数据提供了一种零成本的有效途径。

Abstract: Sand boils, points where water seeping beneath an earthen levee re-emerges at the surface, are early warnings of internal erosion, and deep segmentation networks are increasingly used to find them in inspection photographs. Annotated examples are scarce, and two common ways of working around that scarcity quietly inflate reported accuracy: tuning ensemble weights on the same images later used to score them, and training on synthetic images derived from the very photographs held out for testing. We present a sand-boil segmentation framework that closes both loopholes. Every synthetic image carries a pointer to its real parent, and a per-fold filter excludes any image whose parent is held out; five encoder-decoder backbones are trained under five-fold cross-validation, calibrated by one temperature scalar each, and combined by a per-pixel meta-learner fitted only on out-of-fold predictions. On the held-out test set the proposed Updated SandBoilNet reaches an intersection-over-union of 0.707 over three seeds, against 0.608 for the published original re-evaluated on the same split. Under the stacking protocol the calibrated stack reaches 0.681 against 0.694 for the strongest fold-averaged member, so it does not improve on the best single model; eight meta-learner families reproduce that outcome, which we trace to a mean pairwise error correlation of 0.894 among members. A synthetic pool filtered for label fidelity lifts the champion to 0.718 over three seeds against a 0.707 control. We also introduce a mask-conditioned synthesis route that makes the conditioning mask the label by construction, giving labelled training images at zero annotation cost.

</details>


### [34] [Pictura: Perspective-View Self-Play at Scale for Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26005)
*Yuan Yin,Elias Ramzi,Marc Lafon,Valentin Charraut,Victor Bares,Yihong Xu,Éloi Zablocki,Alexandre Boulch,Thibault Buhet,Andrei Bursuc,Matthieu Cord*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了基于第一视角图像进行大规模自动驾驶自我对战训练的新范式，并开发了高性能模拟器Pictura和策略Alberti，证明了该方法的有效性和零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模自动驾驶自我对战训练依赖于“特权”的向量化观测（如精确的位姿和速度，包括被遮挡的智能体），这在实际部署的、仅依靠自车相机部分观测的场景中存在“表示差距”。常用的蒸馏方法让学生策略模仿其自身视角无法合理解释的特权策略决策，存在根本缺陷。

Method: 1. 开发了GPU加速的多智能体驾驶模拟器Pictura。它在每个仿真步为每个智能体渲染其第一视角图像，从源头弥合了训练与部署之间的表示差距。2. 在单块H100 GPU上实现了高达50万智能体-步/秒（200万图像/秒）的吞吐量。3. 使用Pictura，通过近端策略优化算法进行自我对战，直接从第一视角图像训练策略Alberti，完全不使用任何特权观测信息。

Result: 1. 训练规模巨大：完成了500亿智能体步骤，累计约3500万公里的驾驶训练。2. 性能表现：Alberti策略的驾驶性能接近其对应的、在特权向量化观测上训练的策略。3. 泛化能力：在Waymo Open Motion数据集布局（在Pictura中重新渲染）上进行零样本测试时，其性能超越了特权向量化智能体。

Conclusion: 本研究首次实现了直接从第一视角图像进行大规模自我对战训练的自动驾驶策略。这证明了在训练过程中直接使用与部署时一致的传感器数据（第一视角图像）是可行的，且能通过大规模自我对战学习到鲁棒的策略。Pictura模拟器为解决该领域的表示差距问题提供了关键基础设施，Alberti策略展示了该方法的优越性能和强泛化能力。

Abstract: Self-play in simulation produces robust driving policies at scale. Demonstrations of such behavior have been made using privileged vectorized observations such as exact poses and velocities, even for occluded agents. This assumes that perception is solved and introduces a representation gap with the partial observation of a deployed agent driving from the perspective view of egocentric cameras. A common fix, distilling the privileged policy into a camera-input student, leaves the student imitating decisions its own view cannot justify. Instead, we establish perspective-view self-play as a practical training regime. We introduce Pictura, a GPU-accelerated multi-agent driving simulator that renders each agent's egocentric view at every step, mitigating the representation gap at its source. Pictura sustains up to 500K agent-steps/s (2M images/s) on a single H100. Using Pictura, we train Alberti by self-play with plain PPO. It is the first large-scale driving self-play policy trained directly from perspective images, without privileged observations. Training spans 50B agent steps for ~35M km of driving. It approaches the driving performance of its privileged vectorized counterpart, and transfers zero-shot to Waymo Open Motion Dataset layouts re-rendered in Pictura, where it outperforms privileged vectorized agents. Project page: https://valeoai.github.io/Pictura/

</details>


### [35] [Hyperspectral Intrinsic Decomposition: Joint Recovery of Reflectance and Photometric Components for Non-Lambertian Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25371)
*Hao Ye,Zhan Shi,Chenglong Huang,Tao Lv,Mingjie Ji,Qiu Shen,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于非朗伯高光谱图像固有分解的统一反演范式和双尺度分解方案，解决了现有方法在处理真实世界镜面反射时的局限性，并引入了首个公开的真实世界非朗伯HID数据集CITE和可控数据合成器PISG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱固有分解（HID）研究主要基于朗伯或近朗伯假设，仅有的少数非朗伯方法依赖于简化的镜面假设，不足以处理多样化的现实世界镜面反射，并且通常需要辅助输入或仅能恢复部分耦合的反射和光度分量，阻碍了完整和盲分解。

Method: 重新审视二色反射模型（DRM），提出一个统一反演范式，将四个耦合的反射和光度分量的恢复问题重新表述为两个光谱-空间目标变量的估计。基于此，设计了一个双尺度分解方案：在全局尺度上，利用光度不变描述符作为边缘先验，以保持高保真的固有边界；在局部尺度上，采用镜面引导注意力，对镜面主导区域（包括受裁剪失真影响的区域）进行重点细化。

Result: 1. 建立了首个用于非朗伯物体的真实世界高光谱固有分解公共数据集CITE。2. 开发了物理保真的固有集合生成器PISG，用于可控的数据合成。3. 通过在CITE和额外高光谱图像上的广泛消融研究和实验，证明了该方法的有效性及其在多样化场景中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统一反演范式和双尺度分解方案能有效应对非朗伯效应对高光谱固有分解的挑战，实现了更完整、更鲁棒的分解。所构建的CITE数据集和PISG生成器为后续研究提供了重要资源，推动了该领域的发展。

Abstract: Hyperspectral intrinsic decomposition (HID) aims to disentangle material-related spectral properties and photometric effects in hyperspectral images (HSIs), which is essential for understanding real-world imaging processes and benefits a variety of downstream applications. Most existing HID studies have been developed under Lambertian or near-Lambertian assumptions. The few prior non-Lambertian efforts rely on simplified specular assumptions insufficient to handle diverse real-world specularity, and typically require auxiliary inputs or recover only a subset of the coupled reflectance and photometric components, hindering complete and blind decomposition. In this paper, we revisit the dichromatic reflection model (DRM) and develop a unified inversion paradigm that reformulates the recovery of four coupled reflectance and photometric components as the estimation of two spectral--spatial target variables. Building on this reformulation, we propose a dual-scale decomposition scheme to handle non-Lambertian effects with distinct spatial characteristics. At the global scale, photometrically invariant descriptors serve as edge priors for high-fidelity intrinsic boundary preservation; at the local scale, specularity-guided attention directs refinement with emphasis on specularity-dominated regions, including those affected by clipping distortion. To facilitate future research, we establish CITE, the first public real-world HID dataset for non-Lambertian objects, and develop a Physically-faithful Intrinsic Set Generator (PISG) for controllable data synthesis. Extensive ablation studies and experiments on the CITE and additional HSIs demonstrate the effectiveness of our method and its robustness across diverse scenes.

</details>


### [36] [Gaussian Volumetric Representation for Efficient Shear-Warp Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25377)
*Mayuri Mathur,Ojaswa Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高斯的体积表示方法，通过蒙特卡洛体积估计和课程学习策略，能够在稀疏监督下高效可视化密集医学体积数据（如MRI和冷冻切片），在保持解剖结构的同时实现高达43.86 FPS的渲染速度和11.31:1的压缩比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像可视化需要体绘制算法既保持解剖保真度又维持高渲染速度，但处理大规模密集体积数据集时计算成本高昂。本文旨在解决这一效率瓶颈，提出一种高效且保真的可视化方法。

Method: 提出一种基于高斯的体积表示方法用于高效可视化密集医学体积。优化过程采用蒙特卡洛体积估计，使模型能在高度稀疏的体素子集上训练，同时保持与密集体积目标的一致性。引入课程学习策略，逐步结合结构化切片采样：稀疏体素样本提供体积的早期全局覆盖，切片样本捕获空间相关区域以辅助几何结构和纹理连续性。该表示支持剪切-翘曲等基于切片的渲染方法。

Result: 所学的高斯表示能从稀疏监督中学习多种结构的解剖细节和纹理，显著降低了密集体素处理的计算成本。在多模态医学数据集（MRI、冷冻切片）上，使用稀疏监督实现了高达43.86 FPS的渲染速度和11.31:1的压缩比，同时保持了解剖结构。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于高斯的体积表示结合蒙特卡洛估计和课程学习策略，能够高效、保真地可视化密集医学体积数据。该方法在显著降低计算成本的同时，保持了渲染速度和结构完整性，为医学图像可视化提供了一种有效解决方案。

Abstract: Medical image visualization requires volumetric rendering algorithms that preserve anatomical fidelity while maintaining high rendering speeds. To address the high computational cost of large volumetric datasets, we propose a Gaussian-based volumetric representation for efficient visualization of dense medical volumes without compromising structural and radiometric details. We optimize the proposed representation using Monte Carlo volumetric estimation, which enables training on a highly sparse subset of voxels while maintaining consistency with the dense volumetric objective. In addition, we introduce a curriculum learning strategy that progressively incorporates structured slice-based sampling during training. Sparse voxel samples provide an early global coverage of the volume, while slice samples capture spatially correlated regions that aid geometric structure and texture continuity. This combination enables the Gaussian representation to learn anatomical details of various structures and corresponding textures from sparse supervision while significantly reducing the computational cost associated with dense voxel processing. The learned representation supports slice-based rendering methods such as shear-warp volume rendering, enabling efficient visualization of multimodal medical datasets including MRI and Cryosection volumes while preserving anatomical structures. Using sparse supervision, our method achieves up to 43.86 FPS rendering with a compression ratio of 11.31:1.

</details>


### [37] [Noise-Free One-Step LoRA for Task-Driven Image Restoration with Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25390)
*Jaeha Kim,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 退化图像会同时降低视觉质量和下游高级视觉任务性能。任务驱动图像恢复（TDIR）通过联合优化两者来解决问题。本文提出一种基于预训练扩散先验的确定性、无噪声的单步前向传递方法，并发现适配模块的选择（LoRA优于ControlNet）对性能提升至关重要。该方法能超越传统的多步扩散TDIR基线。此外，研究引入了一种任务保持的GAN训练策略，可在提升感知质量的同时不损害任务性能。实验在分类、分割和检测任务上均取得了优于先前方法的一致改进，并验证了方法在真实退化图像和OCR任务上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退化图像不仅降低视觉质量，还会影响分类、分割、检测等下游高级视觉任务的性能。任务驱动图像恢复（TDIR）旨在同时优化恢复质量与任务表现。尽管近期研究表明预训练扩散先验对TDIR有益，但基于扩散的恢复过程本质上具有随机性（依赖于随机噪声项），这可能会损害任务一致性。因此，需要一种能够克服随机性、同时提升视觉质量和任务性能的确定性方法。

Method: 1. 采用预训练扩散先验进行确定性的、无噪声的单步前向传递，以替代传统多步随机采样的扩散过程。
2. 研究发现适配模块的选择至关重要：LoRA（低秩适应）能带来一致的性能提升，而ControlNet风格的条件控制则效果不佳。
3. 引入任务保持的GAN训练策略，在优化感知质量（如图像真实性）时，通过特定设计确保不损害下游任务的性能。

Result: 1. 确定性的单步前向传递方法在性能上超越了传统的多步扩散TDIR基线。
2. 适配模块实验表明，LoRA比ControlNet风格的条件控制更能有效地利用扩散先验提升TDIR效果。
3. 任务保持的GAN策略成功提高了图像的感知质量，且未对任务性能产生负面影响。
4. 在分类、分割和检测三个高级视觉任务上的大量实验表明，本方法相较于先前的TDIR方法取得了持续且显著的性能提升。
5. 方法在真实世界退化图像和OCR任务上的验证表明了其良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本研究证明，利用预训练扩散先验进行确定性、无噪声的单步前向传递是提升任务驱动图像恢复（TDIR）性能的有效途径，其成功关键依赖于合适的适配模块（如LoRA）。所提出的任务保持GAN训练策略能够在提升图像感知质量的同时，有效保持下游任务的性能。该方法在多个标准任务和真实场景中均展现出优越性和泛化能力，为TDIR领域提供了新的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Degraded images not only reduce visual quality but also impair downstream high-level vision tasks. Task-driven image restoration (TDIR) addresses this issue by jointly optimizing restoration quality and task performance. Recent works show that pretrained diffusion priors benefit TDIR, yet diffusion-based restoration is inherently stochastic, as the sampling process depends on a random noise term, which can undermine task consistency. In this paper, we show that a deterministic, noise-free one-step forward pass with pretrained diffusion priors can substantially improve TDIR, but the benefit critically depends on the adaptation module: LoRA yields consistent gains, whereas ControlNet-style conditioning does not. This enables one-step forwarding that surpasses conventional multi-step diffusion TDIR baselines. Furthermore, we introduce a task-preserving GAN training strategy that improves perceptual quality without sacrificing task performance. Extensive experiments on classification, segmentation, and detection demonstrate consistent gains over prior TDIR methods, and we further validate generalization on real-world degraded images and OCR.

</details>


### [38] [RDVSv2: A Large-scale Benchmark for RGB-D Video Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25392)
*Tianyu Li,Jiahao He,Keren Fu,Qijun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了RDVSv2，这是一个用于RGB-D视频显著目标检测的大规模基准数据集，包含249个视频序列和29,077个带标注的帧。该数据集使用从立体视频中提取的深度图和基于眼动追踪指导的显著目标掩码。此外，作者提出了一个基于Segment Anything Model 2（SAM2）的强基线方法，采用参数高效微调策略，联合处理RGB、深度和光流信息。实验表明，RDVSv2比现有数据集更具挑战性，而所提出的基线方法在多个基准测试中达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-D视频显著目标检测（RGB-D VSOD）领域的数据集在规模、标注质量和深度线索的几何一致性方面存在局限。为了推动该领域的发展，需要一个更大规模、更多样化且更具挑战性的基准数据集，以及与之相匹配的先进基线方法。

Method: 1. 构建了大规模数据集RDVSv2：基于公开的立体在线视频，包含249个视频序列和29,077个带眼动追踪指导标注的帧。深度图直接从立体视频中推导，确保了几何一致性。2. 提出了基于SAM2的基线方法：采用参数高效微调（PEFT）策略，适配SAM2编码器以联合编码RGB、深度和光流三种模态信息。

Result: 实验评估表明：1. RDVSv2数据集对现有的RGB-D VSOD方法构成了显著更大的挑战。2. 所提出的基于SAM2的基线方法在RDVSv2数据集和现有的RGB-D VSOD基准测试上均取得了最先进的性能结果。

Conclusion: RDVSv2数据集和所提出的基线方法为RGB-D视频显著目标检测及相关多模态视频理解任务的未来研究提供了有价值的资源和坚实的起点。研究者已将数据集和代码公开，以促进该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: We introduce RDVSv2, a large-scale benchmark for RGB-D video salient object detection (RGB-D VSOD) with dense frame-level annotations. Existing datasets in this emerging field are often limited in scale and annotation quality, while also relying on less geometry-consistent depth cues. To address these limitations, RDVSv2 is built from publicly accessible stereoscopic online videos and contains 249 video sequences with 29,077 annotated frames. It includes depth maps derived from stereoscopic videos, together with frame-wise salient object masks annotated with eye-tracking guidance. Compared with existing datasets, RDVSv2 is much larger in scale and covers more diverse and challenging scenarios. In addition, we establish a strong baseline for RGB-D VSOD based on Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2). Specifically, we employ a parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategy to adapt the SAM2 encoder to jointly encode RGB, depth, and optical flow cues. Extensive experiments show that RDVSv2 is substantially more challenging for existing RGB-D VSOD methods. Meanwhile, the proposed baseline achieves state-of-the-art results on RDVSv2 and existing RGB-D VSOD benchmarks. We hope that RDVSv2 and the provided baseline will serve as useful resources for future research on RGB-D VSOD and related multi-modal video understanding tasks. Our dataset and code will be available at https://github.com/ltynick/RDVSv2.

</details>


### [39] [Towards Reliable Stain Transfer: An Iterative Data-Model Co-Optimization Framework Based on Multimodal Expert-Guided Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25393)
*Siyuan Xu,Yan Wang,Haofei Song,Lili Gao,Jiansheng Wang,Qing Zhang,Dan Huang,Boxiang Yun,Hongkai Xiong,Qingli Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DMCoStain，一个用于计算生成免疫组化（IHC）图像的数据-模型协同优化染色转移框架，通过迭代优化训练数据和模型能力，在多种组织和生物标志物上实现了最先进的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理学检查依赖H&E和IHC染色，但IHC成本高昂且需要专业知识。计算染色转移是一种高效替代方案，但在像素未对齐的监督下，对异质性生物标志物进行统一且可解释的建模仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出DMCoStain框架：1) 数据优化：采用多模态专家指导精细选择（MEGFS）策略，基于首个模拟病理学家推理的IHC阳性表达（IPE）视觉语言模型（VLM），并为此构建了首个大规模IPE指令遵循数据集ImmunoInstruction（包含150K VQA样本）。2) 模型优化：迭代协同精炼数据和模型能力。

Result: 在多种组织和生物标志物上的广泛实验表明，DMCoStain达到了最先进的（SOTA）准确性。

Conclusion: DMCoStain范式具有很高的实用价值，其提出的MEGFS策略也可作为未来模型开发的专用评估工具。

Abstract: Histopathological examination primarily relies on hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) and immunohistochemistry (IHC) staining. Although IHC provides critical molecular information, it is costly and requires specialized expertise. Stain transfer provides an efficient alternative by computationally generating IHC from H&E images, but remains challenged by unified and interpretable modeling for heterogeneous biomarkers under pixel-unaligned supervision. We propose DMCoStain, a novel Data-Model Co-optimization framework for Stain transfer. It iteratively co-refines training data and model capability, improving staining accuracy and interpretability in both pathological and structural consistency. To refine training data in a clinically meaningful manner, it incorporates the Multimodal Expert-Guided Finer Selection (MEGFS) strategy, built upon a pioneering IHC-positive-expression (IPE) vision-language model (VLM) that emulates pathologist reasoning. To support MEGFS, we construct ImmunoInstruction, the first large-scale IPE instruction-following dataset with 150K VQA samples. Extensive experiments on multiple tissues and biomarkers demonstrate that DMCoStain achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) accuracy. This paradigm offers strong practical value, and MEGFS also functions as a specialized evaluation tool for future model development. Dataset, code, and more details are in https://github.com/SikangSHU/DMCoStain.

</details>


### [40] [ANFI: Rethinking Neighbor Feature Interaction in Person Re-ID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25407)
*Xulin Li,Yan Lu,Bin Liu,Jiaze Li,Qinhong Yang,Tao Gong,Qi Chu,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对人员重识别中嘈杂邻居导致基于邻居方法性能下降的问题，提出自适应邻居特征交互（ANFI）方法，通过建模亲和与差异关系并引入嘈杂关系监督来提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于邻居的交互方法仅依赖亲和关系，其成功高度依赖所选邻居的可靠性；在存在不可避免的嘈杂邻居的挑战性场景下，仅使用亲和关系的交互往往失败，因此需要一种能有效处理嘈杂邻居影响的方法。

Method: 提出自适应邻居特征交互（ANFI）方法：1）同时建模亲和关系与差异关系；2）采用样本自适应加权策略对两种关系进行加权；3）从新的邻域相似性推导差异关系以获取更多信息；4）提出嘈杂关系监督（NRS）训练方法，逐步注入对嘈杂关系的鲁棒性。

Result: 在标准、跨模态和跨域设置下进行了广泛实验，包括与基于邻居的方法和重排序方法的比较，结果表明该方法在各种邻居分布下均表现出优越性。

Conclusion: ANFI方法通过建模差异关系并采用自适应加权和嘈杂关系监督，有效解决了嘈杂邻居问题，提升了人员重识别模型的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: In person re-identification, neighbor-based methods have achieved significant success by interacting with neighbor samples to obtain more robust representations. However, existing methods rely only on affinity relations, causing their success to depend heavily on the reliability of selected neighbors. We find that affinity-only interaction often fails in challenging scenarios due to the inevitable presence of noisy neighbors. To enable effective interactions under noisy neighborhoods, we revisit neighbor-based methods under distinct reliability conditions and propose a novel Adaptive Neighbor Feature Interaction (ANFI) method. The core idea of ANFI is to account for negative effects from noisy neighbors, allowing samples to remain distinguishable from false positive neighbors. Unlike existing methods, ANFI models not only affinity relations but also discrepancy relations, and employs sample-wise adaptive weighting for these two types of relations. Given that capturing negative effects from noisy neighbors differs significantly from traditional relation learning, we derive discrepancy relations from a new neighborhood similarity, which provides more information than pairwise similarity. In addition, we propose Noisy Relation Supervision (NRS) to train ANFI, gradually injecting robustness to noisy relations into the model. Extensive experiments conducted under standard, cross-modal, and cross-domain settings, including comparisons with neighbor-based methods and re-ranking methods, demonstrate the superiority of our method across various neighbor distributions.

</details>


### [41] [Bi-Level Collaborative Learning for Few-Shot Scribble-Supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25432)
*Xiang-Xiang Su,Yufan Ye,Yihang Zheng,Min Gan,Guang-Yong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对医学图像分割中涂鸦标注样本稀缺和监督信号稀疏的双重挑战，本文提出了一种双层协同学习框架，通过超像素模型提供结构先验、生成可靠伪标签，并实现双向交互学习，在少样本涂鸦监督设置下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涂鸦标注是像素级标注的高效替代方案，但在真实临床场景中，可用的涂鸦样本往往非常有限。这导致了监督信号稀疏（来自涂鸦）和标注样本稀缺的双重挑战，严重限制了模型学习完整区域结构和精确边界的能力，因此需要一种能有效利用有限涂鸦监督的新方法。

Method: 提出一个双层协同学习框架：1）上层：引入可学习的超像素模型，为下层分割提供区域结构先验；2）下层：采用基于超像素的区域伪标签传播和空间先验引导的过滤策略，生成可靠的密集伪标签用于分割学习；3）协同机制：下层模型学习的解剖语义会反馈到上层，驱动超像素模型优化，从而实现上下层之间的双向交互与协同学习。

Result: 在ACDC（心脏）和Prostate（前列腺）两个医学图像数据集上，在少样本涂鸦监督的设置下，所提出的框架在分割性能上显著超越了现有的最先进的涂鸦监督方法。

Conclusion: 该双层协同学习框架通过引入超像素先验和双向交互机制，有效解决了少样本涂鸦监督医学图像分割中结构信息缺失和监督不足的核心难题，证明了其优越性和有效性。

Abstract: Scribble annotations offer an efficient alternative to costly pixel-wise labeling for medical image segmentation, yet in real clinical scenarios, scribble-annotated samples are often still limited, imposing the dual challenges of sparse supervision and annotated sample scarcity. These compounded constraints severely deprive models of the structural evidence needed for complete region recovery and precise boundary delineation. To break this bottleneck, we propose a bi-level collaborative learning framework for few-shot scribble-supervised medical image segmentation. Specifically, an upper-level learnable superpixel model is introduced to provide region-structural priors for lower-level segmentation, while superpixel-based region-wise pseudo-label propagation and a spatial-prior-guided filtering strategy are performed to generate reliable dense pseudo-labels for segmentation learning. Meanwhile, the anatomical semantics learned by the lower-level segmentation model under the guidance of the current superpixels are fed back to the upper level, further driving it to learn region-structural representations better aligned with the segmentation task. Through bidirectional interaction and collaborative learning between the upper and lower levels, the proposed framework significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art scribble-supervised methods on the ACDC and Prostate datasets under the few-shot scribble-supervised setting.

</details>


### [42] [Reading Legends on Ancient Coins: An Object Detection Approach for Character Recognition on a Novel Roman Republican Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25455)
*Hafeez Anwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对古罗马共和国钱币的字符识别问题，构建了大规模数据集并应用YOLO系列深度学习目标检测模型，实现了高效准确的文字识别，为古钱币分类与历史研究提供了技术支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古钱币上的文字（图例）对于按时间和发行者进行准确分类至关重要，但由于非均匀放置、原始刻印技术、磨损以及成像条件不一致等因素，识别面临巨大挑战，需要专门的技术解决方案。

Method: 收集并标注了包含5654张古罗马共和国钱币图像的大规模数据集（共38808个字符标注），采用YOLO系列目标检测模型（包括YOLOv3、v4、v5、v7和v8）进行字符识别任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，YOLOv7-Large模型取得最佳性能，mAP50达到90.4%；YOLOv7-Extended和YOLOv7-xl分别达到90.2%和90.1%，展示了深度学习目标检测在古钱币字符识别任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究证明基于深度学习的目标检测方法能够有效应对古钱币字符识别中的各种挑战，高精度的识别结果为古钱币的自动化分类与历史研究奠定了技术基础，YOLOv7系列模型在此任务中表现出色。

Abstract: When it comes to the proper classification of ancient coins with respect to their time and issuer, the textual inscriptions on these coins, also known as legends, are of paramount importance. These legends consist of alphabets or characters still used in English. This paper addresses image based character recognition on ancient Roman Republican coins via a deep learning based object detection strategy. However, legends on these coins pose high variation due to non-uniform placement, primitive inscription techniques, and wear and tear. Additional challenges include inconsistent imaging conditions such as illumination, orientation, and scale. To accommodate these, we gathered a novel large-scale dataset of 5,654 Roman Republican coin images, manually annotated with 21 character labels, totaling 38,808 annotations. For recognition, we use You Only Look Once (YOLO) variants: YOLOv3, v4, v5, v7, and v8. YOLOv7-Large achieves the best mAP50 of 90.4%, followed by YOLOv7-Extended and YOLOv7-xl with 90.2% and 90.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [43] [DensFiLM: Density-Conditioned Video Saliency for Crowd Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25465)
*Anis Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DensFiLM，一种以人群密度为条件的视频显著性模型。它通过一个轻量级的特征线性调制层，使模型能根据场景密度（稀疏或密集）自适应地调整其特征选择和注意力机制，在CrowdFix数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频显著性模型在人群场景中采用单一的注视策略，忽略了注意力随人群密度系统性的变化。具体而言，稀疏场景鼓励追踪个体，而密集场景则使注意力转向集体运动和场景级地标。因此，需要一种能够根据密度条件动态调整其行为的模型。

Method: 作者提出了DensFiLM模型。其核心是在一个Video Swin Transformer编码器的瓶颈处插入一个轻量级的特征线性调制层。该层由一个学习到的密度嵌入驱动，生成通道维度的缩放和偏移参数，从而允许解码器从为每个密度区间选择的特征中重建显著性图。密度条件可以来源于数据集标签或模型自身的预测。

Result: 在CrowdFix数据集上，DensFiLM取得了平均NSS 1.434和CC 0.517的性能，分别比ACLNet基线模型提升了14.7%和14.9%。使用模型预测的密度进行条件调制，其性能与使用真实密度标签的“预言机”设置相当。消融实验表明，显式加入光流（RAFT）和更大的时间/社会力扩展在此场景下并未带来进一步的性能提升。一个中心先验消融诊断显示，在减去中心偏置后，密度条件化相比无条件的基础模型带来了0.462的NSS增益，远高于标准评估下的0.124增益。

Conclusion: 轻量级的瓶颈条件化（即DensFiLM）为人群视频显著性建模提供了一种比单纯增加模型容量更有效的归纳偏置。它证明了显式建模场景密度这一上下文信息对于准确预测人群场景中的注视点至关重要。

Abstract: Video saliency models typically apply a single fixation strategy across crowd scenes, despite systematic changes in attention with crowd density. Sparse scenes encourage tracking individuals, whereas dense scenes shift attention toward collective motion and scene-level landmarks. We introduce DensFiLM, a density-conditioned video saliency model that inserts a lightweight Feature-wise Linear Modulation layer at the bottleneck of a Video Swin Transformer. A learned density embedding produces channel-wise scale and shift parameters, allowing the decoder to reconstruct saliency from features selected for each density regime. The module adds only ~100K parameters and can use either CrowdFix density labels or the model's own density prediction. On CrowdFix, DensFiLM achieves mean NSS 1.434 and CC 0.517 over four seeds, improving over ACLNet by 14.7% and 14.9%, respectively, while predicted-density conditioning matches oracle-label performance. Ablations show that explicit RAFT optical flow and larger temporal and social-force extensions provide no further improvement in this setting. In a centre-prior-subtraction diagnostic, density conditioning yields an NSS gain of 0.462 over the unconditioned backbone, compared with 0.124 under standard evaluation. These results show that lightweight bottleneck conditioning provides a more effective inductive bias than increasing model capacity for crowd-video saliency. Our code is available at https://github.com/aniskhan25/crowdfix-saliency.

</details>


### [44] [Seen, Said, or Forgotten? A Causal Audit of Visual KV Memory Across Dialog Turns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25467)
*Hong Chen,Kang Chen,Yuxuan Fan,Bo Wang,Yubo Gao,Yuanlin Chu,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究质疑了状态多模态助手中基于注意力的视觉信息淘汰策略的有效性，提出了Causal Visual Memory Audit (CVMA)框架进行测试，发现当前注意力机制无法可靠识别未来仍需使用的视觉信息，安全遗忘依赖于对未来视觉信息的低依赖度或已明确陈述的事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 状态多模态助手通常一次性编码图像，但可能在多个对话回合后才回答相关问题。现有的注意力引导的视觉-KV淘汰策略假设当前不相关的信息未来也无用，但未来问题未知。本研究旨在探究视觉信息何时可以被安全地遗忘。

Method: 提出并应用了Causal Visual Memory Audit (CVMA) 框架。该框架采用一种“单次预填充”的配对测试方法，通过禁用图像中的特定区域、整张图像或先前的助手文本信息，来检测这些信息缺失对后续回答造成的影响，从而评估哪些视觉信息对未来回答是必要的。

Result: 在VisDial和ConvBench数据集上的测试表明：1) 现有的注意力机制在排序对未来有用的视觉区域时，其效果甚至可能比随机排序更差；2) 当后续对话回合不需要视觉信息时，会掩盖上述失败；3) 助手生成的文本-KV可以替代已经陈述过的事实的图像-KV，但对于未陈述的事实则不可靠。

Conclusion: 在当前测试的技术栈中，安全遗忘视觉信息的依据并非当前的注意力高低，而是较低的“未来视觉依赖度”（即后续回答不依赖图像细节）或是对特定事实的明确口头陈述。这表明基于当前注意力淘汰视觉信息的策略存在缺陷，需要更深入地考虑信息与未来查询的因果关联。

Abstract: Stateful multimodal assistants encode an image once but may answer questions about it many turns later. Attention-guided visual-KV eviction assumes that evidence irrelevant now will remain dispensable, although future questions are unknown. We ask when a visual fact is actually safe to forget and introduce the Causal Visual Memory Audit (CVMA), a paired single-prefill framework that tests what later answers lose when a visual region, the whole image, or prior assistant text becomes unavailable. On VisDial and ConvBench, current attention can rank future-useful regions worse than random even though a diagnostic marginal-utility control shows substantial selection headroom. Aggregate scores hide this failure when later turns do not need vision; controlled and stock-generated histories reveal a second escape route, in which assistant-text KV replaces image KV for facts already stated but not reliably for unstated facts. In the tested stacks, safe forgetting is supported by low future visual dependence or fact-specific verbalization---not by low current attention.

</details>


### [45] [Safety-Aware Cascaded Inference for Crop Damage Assessment with Controlled Error Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25468)
*José Thiéry Messigbédé Hagbe,Gani Kawsar Gounou,Songbian Karim Zimé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对小农户农业保险中漏检损失比误报成本更高的不对称问题，提出了一个名为CascadeCropNet的两阶段级联架构。该架构通过阈值校准和模型路由，显式地在推理时控制安全-效率权衡，优先保证高召回率（漏检率低），在实验中显著减少了漏检案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在图片式农业保险中，漏检真实损失（假阴性）的成本远高于误报（假阳性）：农户无法获得赔付的后果严重，而专家复审虽增加运营成本但可逆。标准多分类模型优化整体准确率，无法在推理时显式控制和操作化这种不对称的成本结构。

Method: 提出CascadeCropNet，一个两阶段级联架构。第一阶段由轻量级的“哨兵模型”进行二元健康诊断（健康/受损）。若样本的预测受损概率超过校准阈值τ，则将其路由至第二阶段的“专家模型”进行精细诊断。通过阈值选择来校准系统，以满足目标召回率约束（如Rec-Damaged >= 0.95），从而在部署时提供对安全-效率权衡的显式控制，无需重新训练。

Result: 在Eyes on the Ground数据集（23,804张肯尼亚小农户玉米田图像）上评估。级联模型在τ=0.5时实现了Rec-Damaged = 0.974，相比基线模型最多减少了54%的漏检案例。在评估对齐条件下，与基线的表示差距仅为+0.008 F1-macro，表明改进主要源于架构设计而非特征表示。在输入质量下降的情况下，系统倾向于将样本升级（即发送给专家模型），而非自信地给出错误分类，体现了通过架构隔离实现的错误遏制。

Conclusion: 级联架构通过校准路由，能够在可靠性比整体准确率更重要的应用场景中，有效实现面向安全的决策约束。然而，这些特性（如高召回率）依赖于阈值的校准和特定的部署条件，并不构成在任意分布偏移下的性能保证。

Abstract: In picture-based agricultural insurance for smallholder farmers, missed damage detections carry substantially higher cost than false alarms: a farmer who sustained real losses receives no payout, while unnecessary expert review is operationally costly but reversible. Standard multi-class classifiers optimize global accuracy but provide no mechanism to operationalize or control this asymmetric cost structure at inference time. We propose CascadeCropNet, a two-stage cascade architecture calibrated to satisfy a target recall constraint (Rec-Damaged >= 0.95) through threshold selection. A lightweight Sentinel model performs binary health triage; samples exceeding a calibrated damage probability threshold tau are escalated to a specialist Expert model for fine-grained diagnosis. This design provides explicit, deployment-time control over the safety-efficiency trade-off without retraining. Evaluated on the Eyes on the Ground dataset (23,804 images from Kenyan smallholder maize farms), the cascade achieves Rec-Damaged = 0.974 at tau = 0.5, reducing missed damage cases by up to 54% relative to a flat baseline. Under evaluation alignment, the representational gap reduces to +0.008 F1-macro, confirming the contribution is architectural rather than representational. Under input degradation, the system prioritizes escalation over confident misclassification, reflecting error containment through architectural isolation rather than intrinsic model robustness. These results demonstrate that cascade architectures can operationalize safety-oriented decision constraints through calibrated routing in settings where reliability matters more than aggregate accuracy. These properties depend on threshold calibration and deployment conditions and do not constitute guarantees under arbitrary distribution shift.

</details>


### [46] [Agentic AI in medicine: architectures, applications, evaluation, and challenges for clinical translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25489)
*Zheng Tong,Yang Liu,Wanshu Fan,Jing Qin,Zhongbin Han,Haifan Gong,Congyu Liao,Xiaofeng Liu,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 医学AI智能体正从孤立预测转向多步骤临床任务（如规划、工具使用、多智能体协作），但当前评估实践与临床需求脱节，未来需明确的定义、可重复评估和真实世界验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型和多模态基础模型推动医学AI系统处理更复杂的临床任务，但该领域范围尚未明确，且现有评估方法与临床实际要求不匹配，因此需要通过系统综述来梳理和映射现有证据。

Method: 采用范围综述方法，对五个电子数据库进行系统证据映射，筛选了1649条记录，并依据预设标准（如目标导向任务执行、工具使用、外部资源交互、基于反馈的精炼或多智能体协作）初步纳入557项独特研究。

Result: 纳入的研究涵盖了使用外部工具的单智能体、基于检索和外部知识的工作流、多模态智能体以及多智能体系统，应用于医学问答、图像解读、电子健康记录分析、药物安全和临床试验预测。当前证据基础主要依赖公共基准、模拟环境、回顾性数据集和小规模专家评估，对过程可靠性、证据可追溯性、不确定性、安全性、工作流影响和外部有效性的评估仍不一致。

Conclusion: 医学AI智能体的临床转化将依赖于更清晰的领域定义、可重复的评估方法、可审计的监管机制、可互操作的系统设计以及在真实临床工作流中的前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Large language models and multimodal foundation models are enabling medical artificial intelligence (AI) systems to move beyond isolated prediction and undertake multistep clinical tasks that require planning, tool use, memory, iterative correction, and coordination among specialized agents. However, the scope of agentic AI in medicine remains unsettled, and current evaluation practices are not yet aligned with the requirements of clinical use. We conducted a scoping review with systematic evidence mapping across five electronic sources, screened 1,649 exportable records, and provisionally included 557 unique studies that met predefined criteria for goal-directed task execution, tool use, interaction with external resources, feedback-based refinement, or multi-agent collaboration. The included studies describe single agents that use external tools, workflows supported by retrieval and external knowledge, multimodal agents, and multi-agent systems applied to medical question answering, image interpretation, electronic health record analysis, drug safety, and clinical trial prediction. The evidence base remains dominated by public benchmarks, simulated settings, retrospective datasets, and small-scale expert evaluation. Process reliability, evidence traceability, uncertainty, safety, workflow impact, and external validity are evaluated less consistently. Clinical translation will depend on clearer definitions, reproducible evaluation, auditable oversight, interoperable system design, and prospective validation in real-world clinical workflows.

</details>


### [47] [Beyond Counts: A Distributional Robustness Margin For Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25497)
*Clément Grisi,Jeroen van der Laak,Geert Litjens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 病理学基础模型因对非生物变异（如样本制备、染色差异）的过度编码而面临鲁棒性问题。现有评估指标RI存在设计缺陷，本文提出CRoMa，通过样本级距离比较来量化模型在混淆因素下的鲁棒性，并揭示其作为分布特性的模型选择价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理学基础模型在临床应用中，其表征会强烈编码来自不同中心的非生物变异（如组织制备、染色和扫描差异），导致模型走捷径学习，跨队列和机构的泛化能力下降。现有评估指标Robustness Index (RI)存在局限性：其基于计数的公式丢弃了距离信息，且其池化、固定邻域的设计掩盖了样本级异质性，仅评估了依赖于模型的样本子集。

Method: 提出Cross-confounder Robustness Margin (CRoMa)。这是一种样本级、可解释的度量方法，通过直接计算每个样本到“跨混淆因素的生物学匹配”和“同混淆因素的生物学干扰物”的距离差异来评估鲁棒性。它将鲁棒性重新定义为一个队列范围内的边际分布，而非单一的汇总分数。

Result: 在三个基准测试中评估了20个图块级编码器的冻结表征，在另一个基准上评估了4个切片级编码器。结果发现：1. 按中位CRoMa排序在不同数据集上大致一致；2. 底层分布显示出显著的模型内异质性；3. 每个图块编码器都保留了一个由混淆因素主导的低尾部分，其普遍性和严重程度在不同模型间差异显著；4. 这些独特的鲁棒性特征将模型选择框架化为典型鲁棒性与低尾鲁棒性之间的帕累托权衡；5. 更高的CRoMa与监督适配后由捷径引起的性能下降更小相关。

Conclusion: CRoMa将表征几何形状转化为可预见下游捷径易感性的分布鲁棒性读数，为模型的鲁棒性评估和选择提供了一个有原则的基础。它揭示了模型鲁棒性的异质性，并支持在典型性能与最坏情况鲁棒性之间进行权衡的决策。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models are approaching clinical deployment, yet remain vulnerable to systematic non-biological variation across centres. Differences in tissue preparation, staining and scanning are strongly encoded in their representations, enabling shortcut learning and weakening generalisation across cohorts and institutions. The Robustness Index (RI) quantifies whether local representation geometry is dominated by biology or by non-biological variation, but its count-based formulation discards distance information. We show that adding distance weights changes little because the deeper limitation lies in RI's pooled, fixed-neighbourhood design, which obscures sample-level heterogeneity and effectively evaluates only a model-dependent subset of samples. We introduce the Cross-confounder Robustness Margin (CRoMa), a sample-resolved measure that directly compares distances to cross-confounder biological matches and same-confounder biological distractors. CRoMa recasts robustness as a cohort-wide margin distribution rather than a single pooled score. We evaluated frozen representations from 20 tile-level encoders across three benchmarks and 4 slide-level encoders on a fourth. Rankings by median CRoMa were broadly consistent across datasets, while the underlying distributions revealed substantial within-model heterogeneity. Every tile encoder retained a confounder-dominated lower tail, whose prevalence and severity varied markedly across models. These distinct robustness profiles frame model selection as a Pareto trade-off between typical and lower-tail robustness. Higher CRoMa was also associated with smaller shortcut-induced performance drops after supervised adaptation. By turning representation geometry into a distributional robustness readout that anticipates downstream shortcut susceptibility, CRoMa provides a principled basis for robustness assessment and model selection.

</details>


### [48] [Group Equivariant Diffusion for Anomaly Detection in Computational Cytology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25503)
*Swarnadip Chatterjee,Ssharvien Kumar Sivakumar,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对细胞病理学计算分析中恶性细胞稀少、标注稀缺的挑战，提出了一种D4等变扩散框架。该框架通过强制执行旋转和翻转对称性，解决了标准扩散模型在细胞图像上重建不一致的问题，从而在异常检测任务中实现了更稳定和准确的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全切片图像上进行计算细胞病理学分析面临多重挑战：恶性细胞稀少、异质性强，且标注数据稀缺。现有的无监督异常检测框架（包括基于生成对抗网络和扩散模型的方法）主要针对器官级影像设计，需要大型策划数据集，不适用于以细胞为中心的信号。在细胞学中，单个细胞图像的旋转或翻转不应改变其诊断类别，但标准扩散模型会将变换后的视图视为不同输入，导致重建结果依赖于变换，进而产生不稳定的异常分数。因此，需要一种能尊重细胞图像生物不变性（旋转和翻转对称性）的生成模型来进行更稳健的异常检测。

Method: 作者提出了一种D4等变扩散框架。该框架在架构和推理阶段均强制执行旋转和反射对称性（D4对称群）。具体方法包括：1）使用一个D4等变的U-Net网络架构；2）在推理时采用等变噪声耦合；3）可选地使用帧平均。这种方法通过对齐生物学不变性，旨在产生变换一致的伪健康重建，并提高对称变换下异常排序的稳定性。

Result: 在两个公开的细胞学数据集（骨髓和外周血涂片）上的实验表明，与非等变生成基线模型、深度单类模型以及基于多实例学习的方法相比，所提出的D4等变扩散模型实现了更高的AUC（受试者工作特征曲线下面积），并且在前K个预测中检索到了更多的异常细胞。此外，该方法显著降低了旋转和翻转变换下异常分数的方差。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将等变神经网络理论应用于细胞病理学的扩散模型，提出了一个专为细胞图像设计的、尊重其几何不变性的异常检测框架。D4等变扩散模型通过确保重建过程对旋转和翻转的不变性，提升了异常检测的准确性和稳定性，为在标注数据有限的细胞病理学场景中应用深度生成模型提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Computational cytology on whole-slide images is challenging because malignant cells are rare, heterogeneous, and annotated slides are scarce. Anomaly detection frameworks can be trained on normal slide-negative patches and then applied at test time to flag abnormal patches in held-out slides. Most unsupervised anomaly detection approaches including generative ones (GAN-based and diffusion-based), are tuned to organ-level imaging and require large curated datasets. In cytology the signal is cell-centric: rotating or flipping a single-cell patch does not change its diagnostic class, yet standard diffusion models treat transformed views as distinct inputs, leading to transformation-dependent reconstructions and unstable anomaly scores. We propose a D4-equivariant diffusion framework that enforces rotation and reflection symmetry both architecturally, via a D4-equivariant U-Net, and at inference, via equivariant noise coupling and (optionally) frame averaging. This alignment with biological invariance yields transformation-consistent pseudo-healthy reconstructions and more stable anomaly ranking under symmetry. On two publicly available cytology datasets of bone marrow and peripheral blood smears, our D4-equivariant diffusion models achieve higher AUC and retrieve more abnormal cells in the top K predictions than non-equivariant generative baselines, a deep one-class, and a multiple instance learning based method, while substantially reducing score variance across rotations and flips. Code is available at https://swchmida.github.io/D4diffCyto/.

</details>


### [49] [I2VShield: An Efficient Proactive Defense Framework against DiT-based Image-to-Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25522)
*Yimao Guo,Zuomin Qu,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对当前基于扩散变换器（DiT）的图像到视频（I2V）模型可能被滥用的问题，提出了一种名为I2VShield的主动防御方法。该方法通过生成对抗性扰动来保护原始图像，旨在破坏I2V模型的视频生成能力，同时显著降低了现有方法对计算资源的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视频生成模型的快速发展，I2V模型被滥用的风险日益增加。虽然AI生成视频的检测已有进展，但针对I2V模型的主动防御（尤其是预防性攻击）研究仍不足。现有的主动防御方法主要依赖梯度攻击，需要消耗大量显存（VRAM）的GPU来生成对抗样本，这限制了其实际应用。因此，研究动机在于开发一种更高效、计算成本更低、且同样有效的主动防御方法，以保护隐私并防止I2V模型被恶意利用。

Method: 本文提出的I2VShield方法包含两个核心组件：1. 一个集成对抗学习的文本自适应扰动生成框架，旨在减少计算开销同时保持视觉上的不可察觉性。2. 一种无目标的多模态注意力干扰攻击，该攻击利用DiT-based I2V模型的内在漏洞，通过最大化破坏模型内部注意力特征与其干净状态之间的差异来实现防御。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多个数据集和主流的基于DiT的I2V模型上均取得了具有高度竞争力的保护性能，特别是在破坏视频生成的时空连贯性方面表现突出。同时，该方法显著降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: I2VShield为保护隐私、对抗基于DiT的I2V模型滥用提供了一种有效且高效的主动防御方案。它不仅实现了与现有方法相当甚至更优的攻击效果，还解决了计算资源需求过高的关键瓶颈，提升了对抗性攻击在隐私保护领域的实用性。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of video generation models has led to the increasing misuse of image-to-video (I2V) models. Although substantial progress has been made in detecting AI-generated videos, proactive defenses against I2V models remain underexplored. In particular, current proactive defenses against I2V models predominantly rely on gradient-based adversarial attacks, which require defenders to possess GPUs with substantial memory resources (VRAM) to generate adversarial examples. To address this issue, we propose I2VShield, a privacy protection method based on generative adversarial attacks tailored to Diffusion Transformer (DiT)-based I2V models. The proposed method primarily consists of two components: (1) a text-adaptive perturbation generation framework integrating adversarial learning to mitigate computational overhead while maintaining visual imperceptibility; and (2) an untargeted Multimodal Attention Disruption (MAD) attack that exploits the inherent vulnerabilities of DiT-based I2V models, maximizing the deviation of the internal attention features from their clean states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves highly competitive protection performance across various datasets and mainstream DiT-based I2V models, particularly in disrupting spatiotemporal coherence, while substantially reducing computational costs.

</details>
